Nowadays, amid new consumption trends and changes in the                    如 今，在 行 業 消 費 新 趨 勢、新 變 化 的 大 背 景
industry, domestic commerce has shifted from growth-driven                  下，國內商業已經從增量擴張轉向存量優化，
expansion to optimisation of existing resources. The “Involution            同質化競爭導致的「內卷效應」日益顯著，傳
Effect” resulted from intensified homogenised competition is                統粗放型運營模式正面臨投資回報率下滑的
becoming increasingly evident, which poses severe challenges to             嚴 峻 挑 戰。這 種 行 業 生 態 的 根 本 轉 變，正 在
traditional, broad operational models as returns on investment              迫使商業運營企業透過精細化管理加強效益，
decline. This fundamental transformation in the industry ecosystem          並 透 過 價 值 創 新 創 造 增 長 空 間。未 來，本 集
compels commercial enterprises to enhance efficiency through                團 將 把 握 大 趨 勢，融 入 新 消 費 模 式，繼 續 堅
refined management and to pursue growth opportunities through               持「購 物 中 心 經 營 百 貨 化」理 念 及「高 質 量 兌
value innovation. Looking ahead, the Group will seize major trends          現」，深度運營，秉承「一店一策」，努力提升
and embrace new consumption models, while continuing to uphold              項 目 投 資 回 報 率；向 行 業 優 秀 看 齊，打 造 標
the concept of “Department Store-style Management for Shopping              杆 項 目，強 化 本 集 團 的 差 異 化 經 營 能 力，致
Centres” and “Quality Delivery”. It will focus on in-depth operations,      力於轉型為具有深遠影響力的商業管理企業。
adhere to the principle of “Store-specific Policies” and strive to
improve project investment returns. Through benchmarking against
industry leaders, the Group will establish benchmark projects and
strengthen its differentiated operational capabilities, committing to
transform into a commercially influential management enterprise.

     INTERNAL CONTROLS                                                          內部監控

     The Board is fully responsible for maintaining proper and effective        董事會全面負責維持妥善而有效的內部監控
     internal controls and for regularly reviewing the operational efficiency   系 統，定 期 檢 討 該 系 統 財 務、營 運、合 規、
     of the financial, operational, compliance, risk controls, human            風 險 控 制、人 力 資 源 等 方 面 的 運 作 效 率，以
     resources and other aspects of the system in order to safeguard the        保障本集團職責與權力的各自獨立，繼而維

     which in turn can protect the investment of shareholders and the
     assets of the Company.


     The Internal Audit and Compliance Department of the Company,               本公司審計監察部（即為本公司的內審部門）

     review and audit of the finance and operation of the Company and           運審閱以及審計。該部門此項工作的目的在
     its subsidiaries for the purpose of ensuring the internal controls are     於確保內部監控正常運作並發揮其應有的作
     in place and functioning properly as intended. For weaknesses of           用。對於外聘核數師向本公司報告其監測到
     internal controls and accounting procedures of the Group which the         的本集團內部監控及會計程序的不足之處，
     external auditor has identified and reported to the Company, the           本公司充份重視所提建議，並作出相應改進。
     Company would pay full attention to the recommendations made by
     the external auditor and make appropriate improvements.


     During the year under review, the Board has conducted a review             於回顧年內，董事會已不時對本集團風險管
     of the risk management and internal control systems of the Group           理及內部監控系統及其有效性進行檢討，並
     and their effectiveness from time to time and concluded that the           認為年度內本集團風險管理及內部監控系統
     risk management and internal control systems of the Group were             及其有效性足夠高效。
     adequate and effective during the Year.


     The Company has the procedures and internal controls for the               本公司設有關於處理及發佈內幕消息的程序
     handling and dissemination of inside information. In practice,             及 內 部 監 控。於 實 行 時，當 本 集 團 僱 員 得 悉
     employees of the Group who become aware of any events and/                 任何事件及╱或事宜被其視之為潛在內幕消
     or matters which he/she considers potentially inside information,          息，該 僱 員 將 向 本 公 司 指 定 人 員 匯 報，而 倘
     will report to the designated personnel of the Company who, if             有關人員認為適宜，彼將向董事會提呈有關
     considered appropriate, will pass such information to the Board            消息以供考慮及決定有關消息是否構成內幕
     for the purpose of considering and deciding whether or not such            消息並須即時披露。
     information constitutes inside information and disclosure of which
     shall be made immediately.


     The aforementioned risk management and internal control systems            前述風險管理和內部控制系統旨在管理而不
     are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to        是消除未能實現業務目標的風險，並且只能

     absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.                  的保證。

     REMUNERATION POLICIES AND DIRECTORS’                                        薪酬政策及董事酬金
     REMUNERATION

     The Remuneration Committee oversees the overall remuneration                薪酬委員會監督本集團之整體薪酬政策及結
     policy and structure of the Group. The Group provides employees             構。本集團向僱員提供具競爭力的薪酬待遇，
     with competitive remuneration and benefits. The remuneration                並按僱員的表現及貢獻以及行業薪酬水平定
     policy is reviewed on a regular basis based on the performance and          期檢討薪酬政策。
     contribution of the employees and the industry remuneration level.


     The authority of the Board to determine directors’ fees are subject         董事會釐定董事袍金的授權須於股東大會上
     to shareholders’ approval at general meetings. The emoluments               獲股東批准。應付董事酬金由本公司董事會
     payable to the Directors are determined by the Company’s Board with         參考董事職責、責任及表現以及本集團業績
     reference to Directors’ duties, responsibilities and performance and        後釐定。
     the results of the Group.

           intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of             詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未

                                                                                                ）及詮釋）
          the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies                  及香港公司條例的披露規定編製。其亦
          Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical                 按歷史成本慣例編製。除另有註明外，
          cost convention. These financial statements are presented in            該等財務報表均以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈
          Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the nearest              列，而當中所有金額均約整至最接近的
          thousand (“RMB’000”) except when otherwise indicated.                   千位數（「人民幣千元」）。


          Basis of consolidation                                                  合併基準

          The consolidated financial statements include the financial             綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
          statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year             截至二零二四年十二月三十一日止年度
          ended 31 December 2024.                                                 之財務報表。


          A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly     附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制之實
          or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved           體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與
          when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns           投資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享
          from its involvement with the investee and has the ability to           有權利以及能透過對投資對象之權力（即
          affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,         本集團獲賦予現有能力以主導投資對象
          existing rights that give the Group the current ability to direct       相關活動之既存權利）影響該等回報時，
          the relevant activities of the investee).                               即取得控制權。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)                                         2.1 編製基準（續）

      Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                           合併基準（續）

      Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting                  一般而言，假設大多數的投票權導致控
      rights results in control. When the Company has less than a                  制權。倘本公司擁有少於投資對象大多
      majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the Group           數投票或類似的權利，則本集團於評估
      considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing                  其是否擁有對投資對象之權力時會考慮
      whether it has power over an investee, including:                            一切相關事實及情況，包括：


      (a)    the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders               (a)   與投資對象其他投票者之合約安
             of the investee;                                                            排；


      (b)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and               (b)   其他合約安排所產生之權利；及


      (c)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                (c)   本集團之投票權及潛在投票權。


      The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for                附屬公司的財務報表乃按與本公司相同
      the same reporting period as the Company, using consistent                   的報告期間及一致的會計政策編製而
      accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated            成。附屬公司的業績自本集團取得控制
      from the date on which the Group obtains control, and                        權當日起合併入賬，並繼續合併入賬直
      continue to be consolidated until the date that such control                 至該控制權終止當日止。
      ceases.


      Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                     損益及其他全面收益之各組成部分歸
      income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the Group               屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非控股權
      and to the non-controlling interests, even if this results in the            益，即使此舉引致非控股權益結餘出現
      non-controlling interests having a deficit balance. All intra-               虧絀。所有本集團內公司間之資產及負
      group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and cash              債、權益、收入、開支以及與本集團成
      flows relating to transactions between members of the Group                  員公司之間交易有關之現金流量均於合
      are eliminated in full on consolidation.                                     併時悉數抵銷。


      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if               倘事實及情況顯示上文所述的三項控制
      facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one               因素中一項或多項出現變動，本集團會
      or more of the three elements of control described above. A                  重新評估其是否仍然控制投資對象。倘
      change in the ownership interest of a subsidiary, without a loss             於附屬公司的擁有權權益變動並無失去
      of control, is accounted for as an equity transaction.                       控制權，則按權益交易入賬。

      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                 同意分享安排的控制權，這僅在關於相
      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities             關活動的決定須獲分享控制權各方一致
      require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.              同意時存在。

      Fair value measurement                                                          公允值計量

      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset                 公允值乃在市場參與者於計量日期進行
      or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between               的有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉移負
      market participants at the measurement date. The fair value                     債所支付的價格。公允值計量乃基於假
      measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction                    設出售資產或轉移負債的交易於資產或
      to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the           負債的主要市場或（在未有主要市場的
      principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of               情況下）於資產或負債的最有利市場進
      a principal market, in the most advantageous market for the                     行。主要或最有利市場須位於本集團可
      asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market               進入的市場。資產或負債的公允值乃基
      must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset                     於市場參與者為資產或負債定價所用的
      or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market                    假設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼等的
      participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,                     最佳經濟利益行事）。
      assuming that market participants act in their economic best
      interest.


      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                    非金融資產的公允值計量則參考市場參
      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                     與者有能力以最高及最佳用途使用該資
      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by                   產，或將該資產售予另一能夠以最高及
      selling it to another market participant that would use the asset               最佳用途使用該資產的市場參與者所產
      in its highest and best use.                                                    生的經濟效益。


      The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                     本集團使用適用於該等情況的估值方
      the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to                法，而其有足夠資料計量公允值，以盡
      measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable                   量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少
      inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                           使用不可觀察輸入數據。

      Impairment of non-financial assets                                            非金融資產減值

      asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable               的可收回金額。資產的可收回金額乃按
      amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s                 資產或現金產生單位的使用價值與公允
      value in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is                值減出售成本兩者的較高者計算，而個
      determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does not                 別資產須分開計算，除非資產並無產生
      generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those                   明顯獨立於其他資產或資產組別的現金
      from other assets or groups of assets, in which case the                      流入，於此情況下，則可收回金額按資
      recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating unit                 產所屬現金產生單位釐定。對現金產生
      to which the asset belongs. In testing a cash-generating unit for             單位進行減值測試時，倘可以建立合理
      impairment, a portion of the carrying amount of a corporate                   及一致的分配基準，公司資產賬面值之
      asset (e.g., a headquarters building) is allocated to an individual           部分（例如總部大樓）會被分配至個別現
      cash-generating unit if it can be allocated on a reasonable and               金產生單位，否則會被分配到最小的現
      consistent basis or, otherwise, to the smallest group of cash-                金產生單位組別。
      generating units.


      An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount                  僅在資產賬面值高於其可收回金額的情
      of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value                況下，方會確認減值虧損。評估使用價
      in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their               值時，估計日後現金流量按可採用反映
      present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current             金錢時間值及資產特定風險的現時市場
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                   評估的稅前折現率貼現至其現值。減值
      specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or             虧損按其在與該減值資產功能相符的開
      loss in the period in which it arises in those expense categories             支類別中產生的期間內自損益中扣除。
      consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

            Leases                                                                   租賃

      The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                  本集團就並非按公允值計入損益持有的
      (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through              所 有 債 務 工 具 確 認 預 期 信 貸 虧 損（「預
      profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the                  期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基
      contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and                於根據合約應付的合約現金流量與本集
      all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted              團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額
      at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The              釐定，並按接近原有實際利率的比率貼
      expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of                  現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押
      collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to             品或構成合約條款的其他信貸提升措施
      the contractual terms.                                                        所得的現金流量。


      General approach                                                              一般方法


      ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for                   預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初
      which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk                始確認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信
      since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that           貸風險而言，會就未來 12 個月可能發生
      result from default events that are possible within the next                  的違約事件所產生的信貸虧損計提預期
      12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for                    信貸虧損撥備（12 個月預期信貸虧損）。
      which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since              就自初始確認以來信貸風險大幅增加的
      initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses           該等信貸風險而言，須就預期於風險餘
      expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of             下存續期內產生的信貸虧損計提虧損撥
      the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                   備，不 論 違 約 的 時 間（整 個 存 續 期 預 期
                                                                                    信貸虧損）。


      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit                 於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認
      risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since              以來金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著增
      initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group                    加。作此評估時，本集團比較金融工具
      compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial                     於報告日期出現違約的風險與該金融工
      instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default                具於初始確認日期出現違約的風險，並
      occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial               考慮無須花費不必要成本或精力即可獲
      recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                          得的合理及有理據的資料，包括過往及
      information that is available without undue cost or effort,                   前瞻性資料。本集團認為，倘合約付款
      including historical and forward-looking information. The Group               逾期超過 30 天，信貸風險會顯著增加。
      considers that there has been a significant increase in credit risk
      when contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

            Derecognition of financial liabilities                                   終止確認金融負債

            A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under          金融負債於負債項下的責任解除、取消
            the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                    或到期時終止確認。

            and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference between         終止確認及對新負債的確認，而各自賬
            the respective carrying amounts is recognised in profit or loss.         面值的差額於損益內確認。

            amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to        金融負債並於財務狀況表內呈報淨額。
            realise the assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.

            to an insignificant risk of changes in value and held for the            值變動風險很小，並且是為履行短期現

            the Group’s cash management.

            therein for the year are presented in the consolidated statement      呈列於綜合權益變動表。
            of changes in equity.


            (a) Statutory surplus reserve                                         (a) 法定盈餘儲備

                  In accordance with the PRC Company Law and the                     根據中國公司法及於中國成立的
                  articles of association of the subsidiaries established in         附 屬 公 司 的 組 織 章 程 細 則，各 相
                  the PRC, each of the relevant subsidiaries is required to          關附屬公司須按除稅後淨利潤的
                  appropriate 10% of its net profit after tax, as determined         10% 提 取 法 定 盈 餘 儲 備，此 乃 根
                  under the Chinese Accounting Standards, to the statutory           據 中 國 會 計 準 則 釐 定，直 至 儲 備
                  surplus reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of           餘 額 達 到 其 註 冊 資 本 的 50%。受
                  its registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set        相關中國法規及相關附屬公司組
                  out in the relevant PRC regulations and in the articles            織章程細則所載若干限制的規限，
                  of association of the relevant subsidiaries, the statutory         法定盈餘儲備可用於抵銷虧損或
                  surplus reserve may be used either to offset losses, or to         轉 增 股 本，惟 轉 換 後 餘 額 不 得 少
                  be converted to increase the share capital provided that           於 註 冊 資 本 的 25%。儲 備 不 得 用
                  the balance after such conversion is not less than 25%             作 其 設 立 目 的 以 外 的 其 他 用 途，
                  of registered capital. The reserve cannot be used for              亦不得作為現金股息分派。
                  purposes other than those for which it is created and is
                  not distributable as cash dividends.


            (b) Merger reserve                                                    (b) 合併儲備

                  The merger reserve of the Group represents (i) issued              本 集 團 的 合 併 儲 備 指 (i) 附 屬 公 司
                  capital of a subsidiary less the cost of acquisition of the        的已發行股本減去根據重組收購
                  subsidiary pursuant to the reorganisation; and (ii) the            附 屬 公 司 的 成 本；及 (ii) 於 重 組 完
                  capital contributions from the China SCE Group prior to            成前中駿集團的注資。
                  the completion of the reorganisation.
